Rule 1235:
If grade value ?d AND type ClassOfRecommendation, then grade dimension CLASS OF RECOMMENDATION.

Formal Rule:
Head: ?a  gradeDimension  CLASS_OF_RECOMMENDATION
Body: ?a  hasGradeValue  ?d  ?d  type  ClassOfRecommendation
